//イェアソムールrvS16/45/DS43070276.「とりあえず…？」
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLine("<color=#8676cf>イェアソムール</color>", NULL, "<color=#8676cf>伊耶亚索穆尔</color>", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	ModPlayVoiceLS(4, 45, "ps3/s16/45/ds43070276", 256, TRUE);
	OutputLine(NULL, "「とりあえず…？」",
			NULL, "「总之……？」", Line_Normal);
	ClearMessage();

	FadeOutBGM(2, 200, FALSE);
	PlaySE(3, "se051", 256, 64);
	DrawScene("white", 2000 );
	ModDrawCharacter(4, 46, "sprite/riku_warai_", "0", 0, 0, 0, FALSE, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 19, 200, FALSE );
	DrawScene("background/sawa", 1500 );

//陸rvS16/46/DS43020021.「……ごちそうさまでした」
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLine("<color=#4b5264>陸</color>", NULL, "<color=#4b5264>陆</color>", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	ModPlayVoiceLS(4, 46, "ps3/s16/46/ds43020021", 256, TRUE);
	OutputLine(NULL, "「……ごちそうさまでした」",
			NULL, "「……多谢款待」", Line_Normal);
	ClearMessage();

	PlaySE(3, "addse06", 256, 64);
	DrawScene("black", 1000 );

//イェアソムールrvS16/45/DS43070277.「――沈めッッ！！」
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLine("<color=#8676cf>イェアソムール</color>", NULL, "<color=#8676cf>伊耶亚索穆尔</color>", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	ModPlayVoiceLS(4, 45, "ps3/s16/45/ds43070277", 256, TRUE);
	OutputLine(NULL, "「——沈めッッ！！」",
			NULL, "「——给我沉下去！！」", Line_Normal);
	ClearMessage();

	DisableWindow();
	PlaySE(3, "daidageki", 256, 64);
	DrawScene("background/furiker_a", 250 );
	ShakeScreen( 1, 15, 15, 2, 0, );
	Wait( 150 );
	PlaySE(4, "daidageki", 256, 64);
	DrawScene("background/furiker_b", 250 );
	ShakeScreen( 1, 15, 15, 2, 0, );
	Wait( 150 );
	PlaySE(3, "finish", 256, 64);
	DrawScene("background/furiker_a", 250 );
	ShakeScreen( 1, 15, 15, 2, 0, );
	Wait( 150 );
	DrawScene("white", 1000 );
	DrawScene("black", 1000 );

//陸rvS16/46/DS43020022.「ぐへほばっ？！」
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLine("<color=#4b5264>陸</color>", NULL, "<color=#4b5264>陆</color>", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	ModPlayVoiceLS(4, 46, "ps3/s16/46/ds43020022", 256, TRUE);
	OutputLine(NULL, "「ぐへほばっ？！」",
			NULL, "「咕呃噗哇？！」", Line_Normal);
	ClearMessage();

	PlaySE(3, "furu", 256, 64);
	DrawSceneWithMask( "white", "up", 0, 0, 250 );
	DrawSceneWithMask( "black", "up", 0, 0, 250 );

//r襟首をつかみ上げて、沢のど真ん中に放り込む。
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "襟首をつかみ上げて、沢のど真ん中に放り込む。",
			NULL, "我抓起他的衣领，把他扔进溪流正中央。", Line_Normal);
	ClearMessage();

	PlaySE(3, "addse20", 256, 64);
	DrawSceneWithMask( "white", "c", 0, 0, 500 );
	DrawScene("background/sawa", 1000 );

//r巨大な水柱が吹きあがって、青年の姿は滝壺の中へと消えていった。
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "巨大な水柱が吹きあがって、青年の姿は滝壺の中へと消えていった。",
			NULL, "巨大的水柱喷涌而起，青年的身影消失在瀑布潭中。", Line_Normal);
	ClearMessage();

	PlayBGM( 1, "higurasi", 128, 0 );
	DrawScene("background/sawa_01", 1000 );
	Wait( 3000 );
	DrawScene("background/sora8", 1000 );

	DisableWindow();
	SetValidityOfInput( FALSE );
	Wait( 3000 );
	DrawBustshotWithFiltering(6, "cinema", "x", 1, 0, 0, FALSE, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 25, 1300, TRUE );
	DrawBustshot(7, "title02", 0, 0, 0, FALSE, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 26, 3000, TRUE );
	Wait( 2000 );
	DrawBustshot(6, "black", 0, 0, 0, FALSE, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 25, 3000, TRUE );
	Wait( 1000 );
	FadeBustshotWithFiltering( 7, "x", 1, FALSE, 0, 0, 1000, TRUE );
	DrawScene( "black", 3000 );
	SetValidityOfInput( TRUE );

}
